## Prospective dispatch and archive DATA increment

The private measurement also counts the existing serializer's products. It does
not replace `mediaJsonBody`, change ordinary serialization, or authorize a new
profile. Let T be exact serialized UTF8 bytes, Q the peak pending object-reference
cells, S the number of strings at least 65536 UTF16 units, and L their total units.
For a slot, the new `{owner,key,value}` plus its array cell charges C=1, P=7,
N=1 for an array index (otherwise zero), and U=13 plus the retained string-key
units and value units. The slots array adds C=1. The temporary key-vector maximum
is `8+16*length` for arrays or `8+8*members+2*keyUnits` for objects. These include
all enumerated fields even if a later JSON operation omits a value.

Before the first initial-choice RPC, and again before an alternate choice, the
request reserves a separate serializer component. Its pending vector is
`8*(1+Q)`. The remaining component is the maximum of the scan, whole-string and
media phases. The scan holds the slots plus largest key vector. A sub-MiB media
sum returns from the scan before the whole `JSON.stringify` text/wire phase,
bounded by 3T. The media pieces phase includes slots, skeleton, slice text,
encoded-prefix backings, repeated large-string occurrences, all marker strings
and piece cells: `slots + 5T + 2L + 272S + 1024`. Only the `pieces` array is
captured by the returned stream; it does not capture the scan queue, key vector,
slots or skeleton. The streaming bound is `T + 4L + 16S + 16 + 524288`, including
whole encoded backings and the current 65536-unit generator chunk/next result.
The reservation remains held through original upload retirement. Typed views,
stream/controller products and execution frames still require the separate
CONTROL inventory; this DATA classification does not make them passive cells.

A possible content archive is funded before policy hydration can count a choice.
The detached body graph uses the same adopted D formula; its largest scalar's
additional descriptor occurrence is charged separately. The additional 81920
bytes cover both bounded record metadata occurrences and URI-encoded key
construction; 8192 covers fixed-depth descriptor/active-set data and 262144 the
existing 32768-byte archive serializer's chunk/backing overlap. A later policy
that disables retention creates no destination or transferable credit. The
original request charge stays held through the actual archive and other roots.

This increment adds authored refusal-before-serialization and held-original-R2
witnesses. Static syntax/format checks do not execute them. Accounting facts,
execution products and the complete simultaneous HTTP/generation CONTROL table
remain outstanding; this is still not the immutable publication candidate.


The existing accounting helpers also receive positive copy/wire funding before
selection. Their unchanged capsule profile gives
`8*(128+2*512+256+1024)+2*32768 = 84992` logical bytes; the intent profile gives
`8*(64+2*128+64+256)+2*8192 = 21504`. The current attempt component reserves
nine capsule-sized representation allowances (failure/current facts, construction
and replacement facts, pricing/cost staging, prospective event/envelope, terminal
event, and visitor/copy temporaries), eight intent-sized allowances (four legs
and their prospective variants), four concurrent `3*16384` text/wire components,
and one `3*65536` terminal event component: 1330176 bytes. These are funded
representation bounds from the existing profiles, not conversion of source
counters into memory credit. The whole-vertical proof must still close every
producer and CONTROL overlap; the numeric reservation is not that closure.

Chat tool-copy scratch is measured without constructing any array. The ordinary
fee classifier's shallow inventory and, only when the genuine compiled endpoint
has a tool price card, the existing tagged-input inventory are counted separately.
Every copied scalar/string occurrence is charged. Tagged/type/concat/singleton
and numeric-rate arrays use their actual simultaneous maxima, with a separate
bound for the ordinary passive-array type coercion. Their funding overlaps the
already prepared upload at late pricing. The ordinary classifiers and tariffs
are not replaced. Only body/three verdict scalars and final component sizes enter
the retained pricing record; the measurement record and its body alias clear
synchronously before full admission begins.


## Private original-operation failure boundary

The private HTTP owner now supplies a genuine, empty sink capability to the
accounting, ledger, archive and credential-materialization operations. The
capability is checked before namespace acquisition or native work. It does not
replace an Env, namespace, stub, queue or bucket. Ordinary calls keep their
existing branches. The shared soft-budget reader already normalizes original
acquisition and read failures; its implementation remains unchanged.

The first synchronous catch or non-async original-promise reaction creates only
fixed metadata: origin, ordinal, failure flag, primary presence, whether the
primary was `undefined`, and a closed disposition. It does not inspect a reason's
prototype, message, stack, cause, aggregate contents or serialization hooks.
Independent operations keep distinct ordinals even when they reject with the
same object. Successful origin records clear their application references;
failed records remain owned until final request retirement. Original native
promise, stream and signal internals are not claimed as application retirement.

Transport rejection cannot mint a validated budget denial or a selected-grant
refresh error. Budget denial provenance is registered only after the actual
`allowed:false` acknowledgment. Private credential materialization observes
both namespace acquisition and fetch before the existing bounded identity
reader. Only its fully consumed owner response constructs the business error;
the actual bounded Retry-After header is preserved. Missing modern bindings and
ordinary credential operations keep their existing behavior.

Non-OK unused ledger responses start their original cancellation in a separate
custody scope. Recovery queue sends or usage-ledger fallback can proceed while
that cancellation is pending. Recovered failures remain diagnostic records and
do not invent a new terminal business failure. A locked unknown response keeps
funding. Accounting registration reserves its origin before starting work and
joins accepted work even if `waitUntil` subsequently throws. The encompassing
HTTP retirement has a separate entry-funded registration, never a self-join.

Public cancellation supplies a bounded carrier before the unchanged usage
observer. Original response reads, FIRSTcancel and unlock retain distinct
origins. Native errored-reader cancellation shares its read origin only with a
verified unmodified native reader/cancel method and rejected native closed
receipt. A post-native wrapper rejection remains a separate cancel origin.

Authored Node and native tests cover opaque/oversized/undefined failures,
forged refresh and budget errors, actual GrantCredentialObject materialization,
recovery before held unused-body cancellation, R2/pipe failure overlap and
registration after start. Fixture-owned arbitrary reasons stay invocation-local.
These tests are unrun under CODE_ONLY_HOSTED.

Revalidation reader witnesses use the actual DO reply and original native
BYOB reader. A fixture-only hold occurs after its original cancellation settles;
it is not a producer-retirement receipt. Separate read, cancellation and unlock
failures keep their own origins, including `undefined` and a shared opaque
reason. Gate release precedes every host teardown join. A failed signal-listener
removal keeps the request UNKNOWN even if its reader is already unlocked;
removing a fixture obstruction later does not refund that owner.

The new failure records, reactions, scopes, registration products and terminal
aliases still require reconciliation with the complete prospective HTTP
DATA/CONTROL table. Current fixed component reservations are implementation
work, not a completed 1024-slot or whole-vertical payload proof. No new allowance,
profile reduction, default activation, physical-heap or remote-owner retirement
claim is made.


### Operation-local terminal funding

Original-operation metadata no longer needs a second carrier wrapper. A failed
origin is its own frozen six-field carrier, branded in a private WeakSet.
Successful records leave the live-origin Map when their original reaction
settles; a scalar ordinal preserves identity without a successful-operation
history array. Failure/control records remain CONTROL, not passive scratch.

The existing common request debit now prepays each operation's terminal origins
before that operation starts. Each origin's current logical component is 2048
bytes; this component is not a JavaScript object-size claim. The entry reserves
terminal registration and one untouched-response cancellation. The closed
operation reservations are:

| Operation | Prepaid origin records | Last required use |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Ledger fetch | 3 | acquisition, fetch, then original acknowledgment read or unused-body FIRSTcancel |
| Credential/preflight fetch | 2 | acquisition and fetch; the existing bounded reader owns its separate source phase |
| Provider fetch | 2 | fetch and an unused/late original-response FIRSTcancel |
| Queue send / archive put / background registration | 1 each | original outcome or registration after start |
| Archive pipe | 3 | pipe, owned abort, original FIRSTcancel |
| Upload preparation | 5 | listener add/remove, pipe, abort and partial-source FIRSTcancel |
| Caller signal | 2 | listener add and final removal |
| Intake diagnostics | 6 | acquire/read/cancel/unlock and listener add/remove |
| Owned response reader | 4 | acquire/read/cancel/unlock |

These are reserved bytes in the same request charge, not a new arena or control
window. Consuming an already-reserved terminal origin cannot compete for fresh
common capacity. Credits are not released while an original or unknown owner
remains live. In particular, an upload admitted before another request fills the
common arena can still initiate its original cancellation and unlock. New Node
and actual Workerd pressure witnesses exercise that condition; they remain
unrun. This table covers the new origin products only. Complete simultaneous
HTTP/generation DATA and CONTROL qualification is still open.

### Private provider revalidation checkpoint

The private Venice and GMI guards now use a request/attempt/verifier-bound
capability. It is checked before their header/hash work and before either
original namespace acquisition. The Worker snapshot helper and selected-grant
inspect helper return only void completion; neither exposes a graph-valued
promise. Fixed internal receivers accept only the exact parsed graph currently
owned by that capability. The ordinary helpers and DO producer paths stay
unchanged. Inspect sends the ordinary empty JSON object, with no specialized
producer marker.

Snapshot consumption maps the RAW root credential and nested policy record to
the existing record profile. The original complete envelope, unknown fields,
duplicates and discarded occurrences are metered before JSON.parse. Limits
remain 2 MiB complete, 1 MiB per record, 16 KiB whole read backing, 8192 reads
including EOF, and 32 headers/4 KiB. Each fresh RPC has its original response
allowance; this does not reset or refund the inherited admission ledger.

The first non-async observers classify acquisition, fetch, read, FIRSTcancel
and unlock before arbitrary rejection values can reach shared consumers.
Independent cleanup origins remain separate, including rejected undefined.
Native propagated cancellation is distinguished from a post-native wrapper
failure by the original reader/cancel provenance, not reason equality.
Unknown body or lock custody keeps the request and foreground backing held.
Only a consumed owner error response can carry selected-key retry provenance.

The existing full foreground 32 MiB charge also backs these phases. It was
reserved before selection effects. There is no release/reacquire transition or
second revalidation arena. Original materialization and preparation joins
precede reuse of their transient component; the full grant and all unknown
fields remain funded. The policy and derived routing remain additive across
inspect. The implementation checks:

| Component | Prospective logical bytes / lifetime |
| --- | --- |
| Retained grant | Conservative D from admitted containers, members, entries, values and string units, plus foreground ID/command occurrences |
| Persistent guard | Measured identity JSON, verifier/binding strings, environment copies and fixed metadata; retained through last delivery consumer |
| Original response source | 16,793,600 + 4,194,304 + 32,768 = 21,020,672; through read/FIRSTcancel/unlock and source alias clearing |
| Snapshot request | Exact planned JSON text times two plus encoded bytes; through original response cleanup |
| Policy/routing | Separate retained D vectors; routing prefix workspace admitted before construction |
| Revalidation execution metadata | 65,536 logical-byte component; CONTROL classification remains explicit |
| Terminal origins | Nine per snapshot/inspect RPC, prepaid before each attempt's selection: 36 RPC origins plus ten source and two nine-origin consumer roles for Venice (64 prepaid), 18 for GMI |

The grant, persistent guard and active phase must fit the SAME 33,554,432-byte
foreground backing. These component checks are not a completed simultaneous
HTTP/generation DATA or 1024-CONTROL proof. The final inventory must also count
the backing capability, registries, preparation frames, both hash roots,
caller/follower tails, provider validators, routing visitors and cleanup.

Venice preserves dispatch and its existing JSON-delivery or pre-first-data SSE
check. GMI preserves dispatch-only checking and its private variant returns
void. A covered generation hit still performs these fresh provider RPCs.
Environment routing performs no invented inspect. No selection, refresh or
environment rescue is added.

New authored witnesses cover the exact raw record boundary, discarded
oversized duplicates, unknown complete fields, forged-cap zero acquisition,
native cold/HIT RPC counts and ordinary inspect body, fresh canonical changes,
and opaque/oversized/undefined/forged acquisition rejection before effects.
They remain unrun under CODE_ONLY_HOSTED; no native resolution or full numeric
qualification is claimed by this checkpoint.

## Private Venice codec implementation

The codec capability binds the original request, selected upload attempt,
authority and credential bindings, selected model/mode, response descriptor,
actual returned Response and dispatch signal. The private path keeps ordinary
native-response classification and accounting. Ordinary codec acquisition and
read functions are unchanged. No response graph or arbitrary callback leaves
the named codec commands.

One foreground ledger admits `grant + persistent guard + active revalidation
+ codec + generic usage` before each new component's factories. An existing
component is reduced only after its application aliases clear. The following
implemented reservations are candidate coefficients, not a completed fit proof.
`R=2097152`, `F=131072`, `N=65536`; every row also includes the measured header
and selected-model occurrences.

| Codec component | Implemented logical bytes | Retained overlap |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| JSON source/parser | `9R + 24N + 65536 = 20512768` | Whole wire, current BYOB backing, decoded text, parsed data, scalar/descriptor/token transitions |
| JSON output | `2R + 65536 = 4259840` | Original wire and queued/public-consumer transfer; fresh delivery snapshot/inspect remain additive |
| SSE frame parsing | `R + 14F + 24N + 65536 = 5570560` | Whole pending native backing, frame, text/line/data/join copies and parsed data |
| SSE resting/first authorization | `R + 2F + 65536 = 2424832` | Pending backing and frame while original first-data authorization is awaited |
| Generic SSE usage | `3R + 15F + 65536 = 8323072` | Buffer plus predecessor and enforced complete-frame decode/split/parse occurrences |

The generic usage component grows before first byte publication. Before that,
only its metadata component is held; no usage reader can have grown its buffer.
JSON parser/decoded aliases and SSE parsed frame aliases clear before a fresh
delivery authority RPC. The full grant and persistent policy/routing guard do
not disappear at these transitions. Complete simultaneous coefficients,
transient rejection state and all execution products still need final closure.

The private codec feeds `createPrivateJsonBodyParser` one Unicode code point
at a time, retaining surrogate pairs. It shares the ordinary tokenizer and
consumer switch; only its parent representation differs. Its existing depth, node, key and
number limits remain intact. A consumer rejection therefore occurs before an
additional large parent batch can be built. `take()` transfers the result and
`discard()` always clears parser state. The pinned tokenizer's batch behavior
is visible in its [exact dependency source](https://github.com/uhop/stream-json/blob/0f70a661308d12973b63f1e7ec23c396265a5361/src/core/parser.js).
Both tokenizer parents and private consumer references remain CONTROL products; the small
feed does not exempt them or establish a 1024-slot fit.

JSON preserves the 2 MiB wire/8192-read limits. SSE preserves its 128 KiB frame,
2 MiB whole-chunk backing and 32-empty-read checks, without a total-byte or
total-read ceiling. Original raw bytes, duplicate/unknown fields, own
`__proto__`, model checks, errors, headers, events and DONE rules remain on the
actual delivery path. Only the first SSE data frame revalidates asynchronously;
later frames keep the existing synchronous liveness check.

The original read, FIRST cancellation, unlock, abort/listener, pull and retirement
origins are distinct. Only bounded metadata reaches their followers. One codec
child is enrolled before acquisition; a late JSON output enrolls under that
still-active child even if logical HTTP completion has sealed. The output owns
its own consumption/cancellation receipt. SSE joins its active pull and any
first-authority await before closing or erroring. Abort also errors an idle SSE
consumer after the original source joins. UNKNOWN retains all relevant backing.
Neither source EOF, parser completion nor response publication is a release.

The codec and downstream consumers reserve all their terminal factories before
selection. They claim fixed ordinal ranges before acquisition, then materialize
only an actual failed origin at its first observer. The fixed record contains
presence and category, never a raw rejection graph. Successful operations need
no history records. Failed records remain in ordinal order until the final
failure consumer; reason equality never merges separate operations. An UNKNOWN
listener or borrower retains its relevant capabilities and backing. This
representation changes neither the reservation nor source/CONTROL limits.

A codec delivery failure is recorded at its original operation. The fixed
codec retains the exact bounded failure it publishes; the proxy retains the
actual returned-body identity. A downstream native read may forward that
failure only with this body lineage and the original native read method.
Foreign or post-native wrapper failures remain independent origins. No foreign
aggregate is inspected. These three additional owner fields and the bounded
predicate frames belong to CONTROL, not passive DATA. Their aliases clear at
actual request retirement; the complete overlap is still unqualified.

Additional authored witnesses use the real compiled entry and SQLite authority:
55 Node cases, 57 native cases, and one Venice invocation in the existing pinned
OpenClaw UNIFIED client fixture. They include accepted/rejected structural and
wire edges, escaped Unicode, raw duplicate/unknown preservation, more than 2 MiB
of SSE, JSON delivery while archive work is held, and post-native held
cancellation with independent read/cancel/unlock failures, including a 17 MiB
primitive, shared opaque object and rejected undefined. The codec's negative
consumers assert fixed ordered origin/presence receipts instead of hiding
retirement failures behind a successful public error response. These cases
are authored only; local semantics remain UNRUN_CODE_ONLY_HOSTED.

### Repaired object-depth representation counterexample

The former frame representation exceeded the adopted 1024 CONTROL ceiling.
This is a source-counted overlap, not an executed failure or a measured heap
claim. Use a genuine selected Venice grant and a streamed one-MiB Chat request,
then a successful JSON response with `model` followed by `extra` containing
127 nested objects, each with a `child` member. The root plus those objects has
depth 128. At the deepest scalar, all 128 former consumer frames had both `value` and
`key`, and the tokenizer retained 128 parent entries. The wire, node and key
limits permit this shape. Array-only depth witnesses omit the frame-key peak.

Count an execution object, each explicit owner/control field, each execution
index entry and each distinct retained callback separately. Parsed business
graphs and passive DATA are excluded from the following lower bound. Aliased
objects are counted once; different owner reference fields remain fields.
Clearing a reference permits its referenced product to retire; it does not
remove the surviving owner's fixed field from the inventory.

| Simultaneously retained products at the deepest scalar | CONTROL lower bound |
| --- | ---: |
| Consumer stack array, 128 entries, 128 frame objects and 256 frame fields | 513 |
| Pinned tokenizer parent array and 128 entries | 129 |
| Request owner, its four vectors and request/sink capabilities | 28 |
| Original upload owner, capability and failure vector | 18 |
| Revalidation owner and capability | 26 |
| Additive foreground-backing record and capability | 9 |
| Codec registry state | 8 |
| Joined codec source, cleanup vector, diagnostic receipt and capability | 34 |
| Proxy invocation and attempt vector | 18 |
| Still-owned JSON intake instance | 14 |
| HTTP identity owner and its four vectors | 15 |
| Foreground credential owner and dispose capability | 16 |
| Authentication owner and closed authentication capability | 13 |
| Selected owner and closed selection capability | 12 |
| Six completion owners, each object plus six fields | 42 |
| Five still-live origins, each object plus six fields | 35 |
| Two accounting command objects, fields and retained callback products | 52 |
| Venice revalidation state and response-control state | 15 |
| Foreground cache run and its closed command | 16 |
| Three consumption leases and their distinct exact charges | 15 |
| Parser and tokenizer command objects and their fields | 9 |
| Active HTTP operation and its cancellation callback | 8 |
| **Lower bound, before omitted products** | **1045** |

The six completion owners are request, sink custody, upload, codec, preparation
failure accounting and selected accounting. The five origins are terminal
registration, request abort-listener removal, upload-listener removal, codec
output cancellation and codec final abort. In that former representation, both accounting instances remained
captured until the selected operation's `finally`; source completion did not
retire them. The three counted consumption leases belong to authentication,
selection and the foreground credential. No generation-slot tree, registry
index cells, promise/reaction products, active parser call frames, descriptor
transition or upload/account/archive tail is included in this lower bound.

The source rows come from `createJsonBodyParser`, the pinned tokenizer,
`RequestOwner`, `UploadOwner`, `RevalidationOwner`, `OwnedVeniceSource`,
`OwnedProxyInvocation`, `JsonAdmission`, the private credential ownership
records and both `ownedProxyAccounting` results. The existing 128-slot windows
cannot be relabeled to make a simultaneous 1045-product subset fit 1024.

The private consumer now has one direct parent-reference stack and one pending
key. It attaches a child before descent and clears both the pending key and
its temporary member alias only AFTER the actual property/element attachment; the
parent's next member supplies its next key after ascent. It retains the same
own-property definition, duplicate last-wins behavior, tokenizer, token switch
and structural limits. Ordinary callers keep their original frame layout.
The private command exposes only `push`, `take` and `discard`; the genuine
current codec validates ownership before the prepaid factory. Transfer and
unconditional discard clear parent, pending-key, scalar, root and tokenizer
aliases. They do not retire grant, policy, fresh authority, upload or sink owners.

| Private parent representation only | Accepted depth 128 | Rejection at attempted depth 129 |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Consumer parent array and admitted reference cells | 129 | 129 |
| Single pending-key cell | 1 | 1 |
| Pinned tokenizer array and actual parent cells | 129 | 130 |
| **Parent subtotal** | **259** | **260** |

These are parent subtotals, not a full parser or vertical fit. During actual
attachment the temporary `member` reference also remains live. An accepted
depth-128 string leaf reaches 260 parent/key products before both key aliases
clear, as does the rejected 129th-container prefix through a different path.
The descriptor and its four fields remain separate live products during the
original property definition. The 383-product
reduction does not grant new CONTROL credit. Shared factory state, active
consumer/attach/check frames, descriptor object plus its four fields, callback
products, token batches and wrapper state remain additive. Ordinary mode adds
only fixed representation-selection state; it has no private parent array.

The exact pinned stream-json 3.8.0 source and published package match. Its
`jsonParser` holds seven option flags and seven mutable tokenizer-state fields,
its parent vector, `pushStringChunk`, and the returned write callback. The
write activation has the input, token array, nine named scan locals, and its
active match/chunk state. The token array, dynamic chunk records, and returned
`many` object survive until the consumer finishes that feed. In pinned
stream-chain 4.4.1, `many` constructs one tagged object with a `values` field;
`flushable(write)` tags and returns that same write function when final/abort
are absent. It does not create a promise or an extra default write wrapper.
These source facts bound specific products; they do not certify a total.

Fixture-only source observers wrap the original parent pushes/pops, pending-key
assignment/clear and discard boundary. The post-attachment observer first
checks that the actual child property/array element exists, then checks both
key aliases after their clear. It retains only scalar counts. The
Node and real Workerd witnesses assert 128/128 accepted stack peaks, the actual
129th tokenizer parent before consumer rejection, and complete alias clearing.
No instrumentation is present in production. JSON and SSE cases cover object
128/129, a 2048-parent prefix, mixed/wide siblings, empty/escaped/lone-surrogate
keys, duplicate own `__proto__`, 4096/4097 key and number boundaries, and an
8192-unit ordinary string. They compare independent JSON values and original
raw response bytes, model, usage and fresh-authority RPC counts. Native holds
observe parser clearing before first-data authority finishes and while actual
accounting remains pending; complete public JSON can precede the latter's join.
All original released work and memoized body roots still join in teardown.

The complete HTTP/generation phase inventory remains open. In particular, the
former lower bound omitted generation registry and slot trees, remaining
promise/reaction products, active parser/descriptor transitions and concurrent
upload/account/archive tails. Neither these authored observations nor syntax
checks prove their combined fit. Publication remains held; semantics are
UNRUN_CODE_ONLY_HOSTED.


### Unused preparation accounting

The private preparation estimate still runs at its original early point. Its
closed command exposes only failure, close and unused retirement; it cannot
expose its cost, reserve a budget or fork an accounting graph. Therefore this
particular command creates no reservation-plan WeakMap entry. Ordinary
`createProxyAccounting` still creates its original binding and command.

The request and environment and genuine sink must match before the private
facade factory. Its completion is the existing private caller's child scope,
not the encompassing request scope. A positive 512-byte metadata component
is admitted first and stays charged until request retirement. This is a logical
component, not an object-size or complete CONTROL claim. The temporary context
and owner parameter aliases clear before the facade is returned.

Once selected accounting exists, the unused command removes its final flags
entry, seals its never-started accounting child and clears the command alias.
It joins that child's unused scalar receipt, then the caller clears the facade.
The construction flags entry also clears in the factory's private `finally`.
Neither an encompassing completion nor delivery, upload or sink originals are
joined by this transition. No debit is refunded here. A used early failure
retains its actual settlement roots; closing its facade does not retire those
roots, the request or their funding.

| Added private preparation products | Lifetime |
| --- | --- |
| Facade plus three method fields and three method functions | Until the caller clears the facade |
| `command`, `used`, cleared `options` and cleared `owner` captured bindings | Same facade lifetime |
| `retireUnused` promise, await reaction and activation with `completion`/`receipt` locals | Empty-child join only |
| Existing accounting command, fact/flag graph, accounting child and its parent observer | Construction/preparation; actual last consumer determines clearance |
| Temporary own-facts getter call and explicit WeakMap deletion | Synchronous close/unused transition |

These rows replace no unrelated owner. In particular, selected accounting,
its plan callback, generation registries, upload, archive and generic usage
observers still overlap response parsing. The eleven facade/state products
and the empty-child transition must be included in preparation's own peak;
removing the old command from later parsing is not a whole-vertical fit proof.

Two Node witnesses cover an unused command with a held sibling and the real
early-403 settlement with exactly one usage event. The existing native compact
JSON/SSE source observer now counts actual preparation construction and alias
clear, rejecting codec entry while a preparation command is still retained.
The original operations and held accounting/archive joins remain in place.
The new witnesses are authored, not locally executed.

later event fits the unchanged 128-KiB frame profile. No failed operation,
second generation, cache pressure or large result graph is needed.

At that later event, both `ownHttpResponse` instances exist: the inner original
response boundary and the public cancellation boundary. Their original reads
are pending through the codec pull. The generic usage observer also exists.
The earlier JSON-only lower bound preceded these constructions.

16. Total 199. This counts graph ownership machinery, not business DATA or a

The 1037 subset excludes the tokenizer's fixed captured state, eleven parser

The subsequent 22-file implementation adds genuine consumer custody in

The candidate remains incomplete and unpublished. The lower bound establishes

### Same-reader consumer reduction and remaining CONTROL gap

The private consumer now uses the genuine revalidation capability as the key
of one registry. It has no second empty capability or reverse index. Request,
attempt, operation, requested mode, native usage, context identity and request
ID are checked by fixed named commands against their existing owners before
consumer factories. No graph getter or caller-supplied callback was added.

Healthy SSE normalization transfers its actual sniff reader and prefix into
Chat usage. The locked original Response remains invocation-local until that
fixed command constructs the final usage stream. Prefix cells clear after
handoff to the usage/output owner, in original order. No private intermediate
replay stream, Response, Headers or second reader starts. Ordinary replay and
the ordinary `observeUsage` implementation are unchanged. A genuinely mapped
replacement has a different body and acquires its own reader; any abandoned
original remains separately joined. A fixed private clear command drops the
usage closure aliases after all original consumers and the public boundary
join, before root credit release; native stream closure is not release proof.

### Actual returned-unlock and independent closed custody correction

The prior cancellation witnesses held a returned cancellation promise, then
called native unlock and threw synchronously. They did not prove ownership of
work returned by a modified unlock. The private source and consumer now publish
their once-only unlock flag and VOID join before reading or invoking the actual
`releaseLock` method. A native undefined return settles synchronously. A returned
promise or thenable gets an immediate two-outcome observer; only its sanitized
VOID follower and fixed failure metadata remain in the adapter. A synchronous
throw and a rejected undefined return both retain the unlock origin.

Retirement joins that actual work with read, FIRSTcancel, closed and active pull
before testing the physical body lock or clearing source aliases. Native unlock
followed by a held return therefore retains funding despite an unlocked body.
A held return which later performs native unlock remains pending; it is not
prematurely latched UNKNOWN. A final locked body, unjoinable observer attachment,
or other unresolved disposition still holds its owner. Public consumer output,
cancellation and mapped-error publication do not await the separate retirement.
The source's existing JSON validation and SSE EOF cleanup order is unchanged.
The synchronous private adapter initiates observation; ordinary InputRetirement
is unchanged. Hidden work which was not returned is not a claimed join.

The source also owns an independent CLOSED origin. Its join exists before the
actual getter is accessed. Getter throws retain that origin, not read. A modified
closed promise can reject after successful reads without becoming a new public
or billing failure. Its bounded diagnostic remains in the original terminal
ledger. Propagation sharing is limited to this source's original reader,
verified original closed getter and actual native read/cancel methods. Modified
methods/getters retain independent origins. No foreign reason is inspected,
compared for equality, copied into an application record, or traversed.

The incremental factory inventory is additional to the earlier incomplete
HTTP inventory. These rows describe actual products, not spare capacity:

| Reached product | Factory and lifetime |
| --- | --- |
| Source fields | Two new fields: unlock join and bounded native-propagation carrier. They survive until original joins and explicit source clearing. The existing flag word adds two bits, not another object. |
| Consumer field | One unlock-join field per actually acquired reader, including a mapped replacement if it starts. It clears only after its join and final lock check. |
| Each unlock deferred | One Promise, executor function and captured-scope link, resolver function/reference and the existing method activation's resolve binding; executor activation/parameter are transient. The flag is set before the factory and actual method access. |
| Each returned-unlock observer | Actual returned-work reference; at most one Promise assimilation result; two handler functions and their captured links; the chained Promise and two outcome reactions. Native void constructs none of these returned-work products. Actual thenable assimilation retains its original owner; it is not a native-heap exemption. |
| Unlock execution | Source `unlock` method/prototype entry; consumer's existing function. Their active receiver, resolve/following/reader/method/original/catch bindings, actual Reflect.apply call operands and empty argument vector remain CONTROL. Actual methods are never replaced by pinned native methods. |
| Unlock retirement | An additional await/join continuation and its outcome reactions in each already-owned retirement path. The reader/body, closed receipt, FIRSTcancel, active pull and original operation stay owned across it. |
| Source closed observation | A deferred VOID Promise plus executor/resolver products, two original-outcome handlers/reactions and their chained Promise; actual getter/original reference, reader/native-proof/following/catch bindings and call operands. The old single chained closed follower is replaced, not counted a second time. |

Construction uses two sequential descriptor records, each with four fields; they are temporary, not retained source descriptors. Existing shared library functions are referenced, not recreated. Per-read native predicate, actual-method receiver and argument-vector products remain CONTROL until that call unwinds. |
| Independent closed failure | One record with six fields, two Map cells and one ordered-vector cell: ten products when this new origin fails. Classification/lookup/insertion and unwind activations remain additive. The source claims its tenth ordinal at its existing acquisition, not before intervening revalidation. |

Before selection effects, Venice now reserves 64 terminal-origin charges rather

preserves the existing debit. Finishing one role does not delete/refund the
ledger while another original or UNKNOWN owner remains. This DATA reservation

The source's two new persistent fields alone raise the prior 1047 between-feed

### E791 historical draft and accepted-usage admission boundary

The subsequent code-only draft has a private completion-leaf operation, fixed
terminal accounting, four separately owned soft-status readers, coalesced
request/response snapshots and shared private stream commands. Registration
keeps its dynamic scope but enrolls complete work without another child.
Usage settlement, small deferred retention and status checking no longer keep
callbacks in the generic proxy invocation's scope. These are implemented
representation changes, not a completed DATA or CONTROL certificate.

Status readers now observe closed work in a narrow method. Their real physical
retirement removes the reader from its controller. Only then may its bounded
diagnostic records become scalar history; ordinal, origin, primary presence,
undefined presence and disposition remain retained. Final request retirement
consumes that history in acquisition order through fixed scalar diagnostics
before clearing it. A reporting failure retains UNKNOWN. This changes neither
the decision's fail-open/cache rules nor the original joins. The earlier
499-status and 428-terminal component envelopes describe older source blocks
and cannot be carried as bounds for this draft.

The adopted snapshot policy reveals a further source boundary. A genuine
codec's modified downstream `read()` may return bytes that differ before the
first controlled fulfillment capture. The private consumer captures those
actual bytes and the usage inspector must consume them. The codec's earlier
128-KiB frame validation therefore does not bound this later inspector's
accepted frame. Its ordinary inspection limit remains 2 MiB.

One exact source counterexample is the single SSE frame
`data: {"extra":[[],[],…]}\n\n`, containing 600,000 empty arrays. A modified
genuine read supplies this frame before capture; no caller-provided graph or
capability is used. The eight-KiB normalizer cut still publishes promptly and
hands the complete crossing bytes to the same reader's usage consumer.

| Captured ASCII frame | 1,800,019 bytes |
| Unchanged usage wire ceiling | 2,097,152 bytes |
| Parsed containers C | 600,002 |
| Parsed property/index cells P (two per object member) | 600,002 |
| Primitive value occurrences N | 0 |
| Key UTF-16 units U | 5 |
| Graph alone: `8(C+P+N)+2U` | 9,600,042 logical bytes |
| Current usage component: `3×2 MiB + 15×128 KiB + 65,536` | 8,323,072 logical bytes |
| Graph-only shortfall | 1,276,970 logical bytes |

`createSseUsageInspector` in `token-usage.ts`. The current private caller cannot

different bytes; applying a new 128-KiB usage ceiling would reduce the profile.

The smallest requested additional source boundary is a genuine private

## Accepted-byte usage draft (source 25)

The private consumer now uses the shared `token-usage.ts` evidence parser and
accumulator. Ordinary callers keep their existing API, returned layout and
arbitrary-JavaScript accumulator behavior. The new registry accepts only the
genuine consumer's current response, mode and successive captured snapshot.
Native `JSON.parse` remains the grammar and value constructor. Capacity checks
are outside its syntax catches; a failed accepted-frame operation is terminal
and cannot be replayed by the unfinished-frame cleanup path.

A scalar census charges every container opener C, quoted token S and maximal
non-string atom A. Quoted raw content supplies the decoded-UTF16 upper U,
including escape spellings and unfinished strings. The prospective graph
component is `16(C+S+A)+2U`. This covers scalar strings, duplicate/discarded
members and malformed-prefix construction. It does not impose the codec's
128-depth limit on the separate native usage parser.

| Live DATA component | Admission and actual retirement boundary |
| --- | --- |
| Accepted read | Original whole backing, capture and possible output copies are admitted before the first controlled fulfillment copies bytes. Only the actual joined read and cleared aliases permit a smaller capture component. UNKNOWN retains its high-water debit. |
| SSE framing | Old and replacement buffers overlap during growth. Completed frame text is decoded before the frame buffer is replaced by the next 8-KiB buffer; both backings are funded during replacement. Pending-frame inspection retains its buffer. |
| SSE line fold | Scalar ranges replace the private split/filter/map vectors. The complete frame and old/new data concatenations remain admitted until the actual local aliases clear. Ordinary trimming and event-error precedence remain unchanged. |
| Native JSON graph | Census precedes `JSON.parse`; text, graph and extraction/update intermediates overlap. The graph charge lasts through evidence acceptance and extraction, not merely through parse return. |
| JSON accumulation | Decoder output and old/new accumulated text overlap before assignment. Final decoder flush and graph admission occur before the native parser. The existing 2-MiB usage ceiling remains. |
| Retained message summary | Only the private retained `stop_reason` becomes `"refusal"` or an own `undefined` field. Missing delta retains it; a present non-refusal/null value clears it. Current evidence is still complete for mixed-protocol and rejection decisions. |
| Codec versus usage | Independent updates preserve the other component. Codec EOF cannot zero live usage. Final usage retirement requires the actual consumer/public/accounting joins. |

The wide 1,800,019-byte fixture's actual graph lower is 9,600,042 logical
bytes under the two-cell object-member convention. Its census reservation is
9,600,058. The prior 8,323,072 usage allocation is no longer used. These are

UNKNOWN keeps the operation and its history. Compaction preserves all ordinal
facts in two CONTROL Map cells; it releases no DATA or capacity credit.

The unchanged status, history, registration, accounting-receiver and leaf
blocks carry their source component inventories. The revised terminal adds an
own observation resolver field, deferred Promise and resolver; its old pending
and tail expressions therefore require this delta. Sixteen retired status and

actual tails and last carrier consumers have ended. Live carriers still cost

Four status reads can still overlap a later accepted depth128 SSE frame after
the 50-ms grace. The carried status storage/observer envelope is at most372
before active parser/factory calls; the parser component remains415 with its
shared281 slice charged once. Their sum is not an allocation across the four

operation/source/read/terminal128 and synchronous512 assignment is still open;
this draft grants no runtime or publication qualification.

At frozen5879 the next bounded design decision was the codec's materializing parent stack:
it is128 of the258 depth products. A private validation-only consumer of the

### Private codec validation and permanent shared custody

The private Venice codec now calls the one-shot `validatePrivateJsonBody`
command. Ordinary `createJsonBodyParser` and expanded-ingress
`createPrivateJsonBodyParser` are unchanged. The new validator retains six
scalar counters/modes and the same pinned tokenizer. It constructs no result
containers, parent-container vector, key values, scalar values or escaping
facade. Depth is checked before the value count. Key and number chunk lengths
retain the 4096 limit; ordinary string values do not acquire that limit.

For valid text, `V` counts every value, `K` every key and `U` every decoded
UTF-16 string unit, including discarded duplicates. The returned scalar is
`G = 8 * (2V - 1 + K) + 2U`. Its finally clears the tokenizer, callback and text
aliases. The complete helper invocation returns before codec graph admission
and native `JSON.parse` of the exact original text. Native parsing has no
reviver, trim, normalization or intervening await. The caller retains and
funds the text separately. Invalid validation never reaches the native graph
factory. Graph admission remains outside syntax catches.

The codec charges its validation-phase wire/text/copy envelope before the validator:
JSON uses `6R + 65536`; SSE uses `2R + 14F + 65536`, in addition to its existing
header/model occurrences. It then adds actual `G` before native parsing.
These are component reservations, not a whole foreground 32 MiB certificate.
Grant, persistent policy/routing, fresh revalidation, accepted usage and
consumer backing remain additive. Codec phase replacement does not reset or
release the independent accepted-usage debit.

The first genuine started codec with its current source claims 65536 bytes
through the same canonical `ExactBodyCharge` imported from `body-budget.ts`.
The request root, preflight arena and this claim import that same module; the
existing 96 MiB counter is used without a replacement namespace, arena or
counter. The claim is immediately published in one module singleton, with no
await, callback or table construction between reserve and publication.
Insufficient capacity leaves the counter unchanged and starts no validator.
Subsequent requests borrow the existing claim without another reservation.

This is normative custody transfer of an already-loaded fixed dependency
slice, not prospective admission of ES-module initialization. The guard is in
response parsing, after provider dispatch; first-claim refusal therefore has
that post-dispatch timing. Ordinary routes and expanded ingress do not activate
it. It remains charged through parser failure and all request retirements,
until the actual Worker module/isolate terminates. There is no private release
hook or counter reset. Its permanent 65536 debit reduces COMMON headroom for
ordinary competing traffic; unchanged ordinary APIs do not imply unchanged
available headroom.

### Private status transition draft

The four private status readers now run fixed named transitions under the
existing controller retirement receipt. Their pending bits are published
before actual fetch/read/FIRSTcancel/unlock/closed access and invocation.
Actual first callbacks clear only their own bit and enqueue the fixed receiver
through `queueMicrotask`; the queued transition runs after the callback and
synchronous observer installation return. The returned value of modified
`.then` is never used as a settlement receipt. Serial-bound read callbacks
cannot satisfy a later reused read slot. Queue/installation uncertainty keeps
UNKNOWN and the original funding.

An installation throw does not revoke an already-started fetch callback. A
late response still enters the same owner; cleanup acquires its actual body
and starts FIRSTcancel only once that body exists. An earlier empty-body
placeholder cannot consume that cancellation. Observation uncertainty remains
UNKNOWN after these physical cleanup attempts.

This removes `acquire()`/`run()` async continuations, run-observer products and
logical graph-valued read-result Promises. Decoder, backing, complete text,
wire/read counters, received view and scalar decision move into explicit
fields; those fields still count. One bound transition receiver and its queued
work also count. Read, FIRSTcancel and returned unlock still precede the cache
or denial decision. Independent closed observation stays diagnostic and joins
physical retirement afterward. The unchanged decision owner retains the 50 ms
race, original lookup timing, completion-arrival order, late cache updates and
close suppression. No status fetch receives a new abort signal.

Source observers now distinguish scalar validator depth from actual tokenizer
parent cells and assert no native parse before unconditional validator alias
clear. Existing private materializer attachment observers remain for expanded
ingress. New authored checks cover native parse/failure boundaries, scalar and
numeric semantics, duplicate node limits, strict whitespace, wire BOM,
per-instance first claim, concurrent reuse, permanent baseline, failed-claim
counter stability and exact request retirement. Seven new top-level tests are
authored, plus three codec cases in the existing JSON/SSE loops. All semantics
remain UNRUN_CODE_ONLY_HOSTED. No whole DATA or individual CONTROL-window
certificate follows from these component changes.

### Fixed reader driver and canonical private owners: source-only checkpoint

This draft implements the selected layout as one unit. It preserves the source,
consumer and public native stream/Response/reader boundaries. `OwnedReaderDriver`
in `proxy-completion.ts` holds their fixed pending masks, read/pull serials,
observer-installation depth, active callback tails and queued transition.
Ordinary `ProxyCompletion`, including the private-leaf method, remains byte
identical after removing the added import and appended private tail.

A read now resolves a void readiness receipt. Its actual result stays in the
originating reader's captured-byte/done slots. Consumer and source capture read
`value` before `done`; the public boundary reads `done` before `value`. Actual
method/getter receivers, arguments and calls remain observable. Each observer
publishes its pending state before invocation. Duplicate callbacks cannot
consume a slot twice; stale read and pull serials cannot consume a later slot.
A modified `.then` return is ignored as evidence. Callback completion and
observer-installation completion precede readiness. Installation uncertainty
keeps UNKNOWN even after a synchronous callback or later native settlement.

FIRSTcancel, returned unlock, independent closed and active pull use the same
driver, without individual joining deferreds. The driver still retains every
actual pending original and observation. The sniff's immediate unlock path is
separate from normal read/cancel-before-unlock retirement. Public error/cancel
and accounting decisions do not await physical retirement. The public reader
has one retirement leaf enrolled directly in the request parent before open;
it has no enclosing completion child or per-pull leaf. Its pull still returns
its actual void work Promise. A completed pull observer can be consumed before
a native stream's next demand without losing the already queued transition.

The private usage instance now combines `OwnedAcceptedUsage` and `OwnedChatUsage`
state through one concrete instance in `token-usage.ts`. The shared framer,
evidence parser and accumulator algorithms remain. The canonical instance owns
mode/context, snapshot epoch, final inspection and delivery state; the existing
indexes point to that instance. Those indexes, inherited fields and forwarding
receipt still cost CONTROL. The private status instance similarly combines
`OwnedSoftBudget` and `OwnedStatusController`. Logical check and physical
retirement remain distinct. Its four readers, post-dispatch binding/config
lookup, cache decisions, grace race and late responses are retained. The proxy
uses named void/check/decision commands; no plan getter leaves the private
status owner. Deferred generation remains unchanged.

claim. Native function targets are borrowed; their new reference cells count.
The guard is not a capability check and is never called by an ordinary route.

### Flat private census and native construction checkpoint

This draft replaces only the private codec tokenizer validator. The ordinary
and expanded-ingress factories and their shared materializer remain byte-identical.
`measurePrivateJsonBody` is one allocation/limit scanner used by the private
codec and accepted-usage owner. Its only result is `{ upper, exact, limited }`.
Each fixed caller consumes and clears that receipt before native construction.
The scanner clears its text parameter and exits completely. It constructs no
parent vector, token vector, decoded string, callback, facade or Promise.

The allocation expression is always `16*(C+S+A)+2*Uraw`, including partial atoms,
unfinished strings, apparent keys and discarded duplicates. K is never subtracted
before grammar succeeds. Allocation atom separators preserve the former private
usage scanner's broad whitespace set, including VT/FF, NBSP, Unicode spaces and
BOM. Key lookahead uses only JSON's four whitespace characters. Neither rule
accepts grammar: native `JSON.parse` receives the exact original text.

The separate valid-only expression is `8*(2V-1+K)+2*Udecoded`, with
`V=C+S-K+A`. The codec uses it only after successful native parsing. Quoted keys
are classified before the value limit verdict, so a valid final key cannot
provisionally push the value count above65536. Container depth is checked before
its value count. Short escapes and valid Unicode escapes each contribute one
decoded UTF-16 unit; surrogate pairs contribute two. Keys and number spellings
retain4096; ordinary value strings have no such cap. Usage ignores `limited`,
retaining its existing broader native-JSON profile and accumulator semantics.

| SSE |7667712|6094848|1310728|7405576|

Header funding is additive to each row. The full parsing component remains
paid through the census and native constructor; there is no shrink/reacquire
window. A limited verdict or an actual `backing+upper` exceeding that already-paid
component rejects locally before native construction. The valid-profile bounds
exclude valid admitted wire from this latter branch. No upper is clamped and
then used to parse larger input. After native success, the codec replaces its
component with `backing+exact` before fresh authorization or later usage work.
Independent usage and consumer debits remain untouched. These are component
bounds, not the whole foreground32MiB or COMMON96MiB proof.

Only the native constructor is inside its syntax catch. A native SyntaxError
alias clears synchronously before creating the fixed local `Error("invalid owned
JSON")`; native input/message/cause/stack is not carried into cleanup. Capability
and genuine capacity failures remain outside that catch. The adopted malformed
priority change allows flat limits before native grammar, and allows malformed

phase-specific abort, capacity and soft-denial precedence remains. Prepublication
upstream-format failure remains502/provider_unavailable; published SSE keeps its
status and fails its body. No universal503 claim follows. Original cleanup,
actual failure ordinals, reservation/event IDs and fresh-RPC ordering remain.

### Scope27 draft: bounded private ledger acknowledgments

This in-progress source-only draft replaces the two private ledger
`Response.json()` construction paths with one closed byte consumer. The ordinary
ledger callers and DO response producers retain their existing behavior. The
expanded boundary also permits narrow private receipt adapters in
`credential-preflight-source.ts` and `identity-admission.ts`. The draft routes
private acquisition through those adapters; their complete inventory and source
qualification remain open. No runtime qualification is claimed.

Each private reserve, dispatch, charge, settle and ingest acknowledgment permits
32768 complete wire bytes and 32768 decoded UTF-16 units. Each actual read result
may retain at most a 65536-byte whole ArrayBuffer. BYOB uses a 16384-byte working
view; a genuine default reader is a parsing fallback. There are no additional
read-count, empty-read, depth, node, key or number-spelling limits. Unknown fields
are parsed completely within these bounds. The decoder uses replacement UTF-8,
consumes an initial BOM, preserves byte-bearing EOF, and flushes once.

The shared flat census supplies only its malformed-inclusive allocation upper,
`16*(C+S+A)+2*Uraw`. Its codec-specific limit verdict does not restrict ACKs. The
census receipt and scanner scope end before `JSON.parse` constructs the exact
decoded text without a reviver or trimming. Native syntax failures become bounded
local facts; native messages, input, causes and stacks do not cross cleanup waits.
Genuine admission failures stay outside syntax normalization.

The prospectively reserved reader component is1098240 bytes:65536 returned
backing,16384 BYOB working backing,65536 private capture,32768 retained wire,
262144 for up to four UTF-16 text occurrences,589824 malformed-inclusive graph
upper, and66048 projected-reply allowance. This conservative component is not a
whole-request bound or a new quota. Metadata, actual execution products and every
other live ACK, source, upload, parser, usage and accounting owner remain additive.
The implementation creates no extra header iterator or copied header profile.

After EOF or failed-read disposition, payload credit reduces only after the
actual read and cancellation callbacks, installation barriers and tails have
joined and every capture/text/decoder/parsed-value alias has cleared. Independent
closed and returned-unlock custody remains held. UNKNOWN prevents that reduction.
A closed reply retained by a nonterminal caller keeps its projected allowance
through request retirement.

Only a genuine `allowed:false` reserve acknowledgment produces the existing402.
Oversize, malformed or unsupported reserve replies use failed-reserve rollback;
dispatch remains unavailable; charge and settle retain their existing recovery
and IDs; ingest never falsely confirms storage. Non-OK404/409 paths remain
status-driven and do not parse error bodies. Fetch/acquisition rejection remains
separate from invalid-ACK normalization. Historical or directly written reserve
policies whose replies exceed the new private profile intentionally fail ACK.

The authored Node cases cover all five scalar replies, W/W+1 wire, B/B+1 whole
backing, deep unknown values, long keys/numbers, BOM, duplicates, own `__proto__`,
lone surrogates and transport-versus-invalid-ACK routing. The old large-array
counterexample is arithmetic only:2097152 empty objects imply6291483 wire bytes
and33554514 graph bytes; no large fixture is constructed. These cases remain
UNRUN_CODE_ONLY_HOSTED. Workerd/client additions and complete phase/window/shared
inventories remain open. Earlier checkpoint lower bounds are historical, not
upper bounds for this changed representation.

### Scope27 draft: private callback acceptance

Each genuinely started private original has one callback acceptance right. The
owner checks its live pending state and operation nonce before inspecting a value
or reason, then consumes the right before any payload getter, validation, copy,
diagnostic or scheduling call. A protocol-invalid first value consumes the same
right. A later callback cannot repair or replace it. Independent originals retain
independent rights and failure ordinals, even when their reasons are identical or
undefined.

Logical cancellation and uncertain observer installation do not erase a pending
original. Its first late Response is still adopted and disposed through the
original owner's FIRSTcancel and physical joins. A synchronous first callback
followed by an installation throw records both events and keeps UNKNOWN. Only
successful installation, the first callback, its tail and all actual child joins
can permit retirement.

After the first accepted callback, duplicate, stale and post-retirement values
remain foreign producer payloads. The private callback returns void without
accessing their body, headers, result getters or reasons. It does not cancel a
second Response, allocate another child, reopen a registry or alter the outcome.
This boundary makes no claim that those foreign payloads were cleaned or retired.
Live saved callback references and their captured state still require explicit
custody accounting. A harmless stale invocation alone does not establish
retirement. The separate inert-artifact rule below requires actual detachment.

The separate private observer-return policy discards the actual `.then`
invocation return without reading, assimilating or observing it. An unrelated
held Promise, undefined return or hostile returned object supplies neither a
business result nor retirement evidence. External rejected-return reporting is
not suppressed. Ordinary APIs retain their existing behavior.

The added authored witnesses exercise duplicate ledger Responses during physical
cleanup and after request retirement, rejection with undefined followed by a
foreign fulfillment, and result getters that reenter the same read callback.
They remain UNRUN_CODE_ONLY_HOSTED. Complete CONTROL closure is still pending;
these behavioral witnesses do not supply a window or external-retention bound.

### Scope27 draft: retired inert observer artifacts

The private accounting domain explicitly excludes a discharged inert observer
artifact retained solely by an external observer, and external calls into that
inert artifact. This is a custody transfer, not garbage-collection evidence or a
bound on external/native heap use. Active factories and live cells remain on the
originating ticket's existing windows. No request receives extra capacity.

The reader driver now supplies two fixed functions bound only to a fresh mutable
cell. The cell has four own fields: owner, token, kind and state. Each operation
gets a distinct cell; no cell is reused and no historical-cell collection exists.
The driver has five current-operation positions, including READ and PULL. Each
cell contributes its object and four fields, two bound functions and their target
and receiver references, plus the actual reactions, native invocation products
and active operands. The pre-existing readiness Promise remains one actual
product, counted at its surviving owner; moving its reference into a cell does
not erase it. These are component facts, not a complete window upper.

Discharge requires the first eligible callback, successful installation, ended
callback/installation tails, completed operation bookkeeping, and no UNKNOWN.
Any captured value or bounded failure has already moved into the still-funded
reader before the cell loses its owner and nonce links. The cell is marked inert,
all graph links are cleared, and its private driver position is cleared before
reuse. A queued driver pump retains the driver, not an earlier cell or nonce; its
own products stay funded. PULL may complete this same bookkeeping at the next
native demand boundary before rearming. The cell's old callbacks then return
without consulting a parent, payload, registry or diagnostics.

The private preflight and materialization adapters, status reader and ledger ACK
reader use separate narrow READ cells for their reusable observations. They clear
those cells only after consuming or discarding that read's captured bytes and
completing its observation barriers. UNKNOWN leaves the cell attached. Shared
handler definitions belong in the permanent fixed-custody inventory; cell fields,
bound handlers, state positions, receipts and calls belong to the active ticket.
The inventory for all other retained observers remains required.

Authored driver witnesses retain 148 and 512 successful READ/PULL callback pairs,
invoke old callbacks during later reads and after retirement, and inspect the
cells' cleared owner/nonce links. A separate installation-throw witness requires
the cell to remain attached. These tests are UNRUN_CODE_ONLY_HOSTED; they neither
measure garbage collection nor certify the whole DATA/CONTROL bound.


### Scope27 intake cells and earlier permanent custody

The private intake now claims the same permanent 65536-byte fixed module slice
in `beginOwnedHttpIntakeDiagnostics`, after genuine request validation and the
positive request/origin reservations, before its diagnostic receipt, I/O owner,
capture backing or observer cells. The prior response-stage claim descriptions
above are historical checkpoints. Private 401, malformed and declared-size
failures can now leave this permanent claim. It reduces COMMON headroom for
later authentication and ordinary traffic; there is no release or reset.
Ordinary `readJson`, default Worker routes and module imports do not activate it.

A failed first claim constructs no intake owner or reader. It stays inside the
existing `proxyOwnedHttpChat` intake catch: authentication still runs before the
intake error is rethrown. Missing credentials can therefore win with 401, and
authentication capacity failure keeps its own precedence. This is not an
unconditional new 503 response. Request retirement remains exact, separately
from the permanent shared claim.

`OwnedHttpIntakeIo` receives only the genuine capability, diagnostics and actual
body. The async intake driver retains the parser, admission, prefix and parsed
graph. Native read, FIRSTcancel, returned unlock and independent closed handlers
bind only fresh, never-reused cells. A read cell remains live through any
admission/authentication await until its captured bytes are copied or consumed.
A queued receipt resolves only after the first callback and successful observer
installation. UNKNOWN retains its attached cell and funding. Normal consumption
severs the cell's owner, kind, readiness and resolver before the next read; it
does not claim that externally retained callbacks cease to exist.

Intake observes `!!item.done` first. A truthy result skips VALUE entirely,
including byte-bearing EOF. Otherwise it preserves the three conditional VALUE
getter accesses: backing limit, length, then capture copy. The first callback
right is consumed before any getter can reenter. The same private 16KiB capture
slab, growing prefix, zero-read skip, parser thresholds and decoder flush remain.
Cleanup still starts FIRSTcancel before joining read/cancel, removes the abort
listener, joins returned unlock and independent closed, and checks lock disposition
before return. Observer-installation failures have independent bounded origins.

Authored actual Workerd witnesses retain more than148 and512 read callback pairs,
reenter through DONE/VALUE, use truthy DONE with a hostile VALUE getter at EOF,
and invoke all saved handlers after retirement. They inspect severed cell links
and exact getter order, not GC. Earlier native mutation/authentication-await,
late-abort, returned-unlock and independent-closed cases use the new owner hooks.
New cold-intake 401/malformed/header and failed-claim witnesses check exact permanent
custody and zero unfunded I/O factories. All remain **UNRUN_CODE_ONLY_HOSTED**.
The added owner, five-field cell, receipt/resolver, bound handlers, queued advance,
active installations and fixed definitions require the new actual inventory;
this section makes no DATA or CONTROL fit claim.

The driver has38 concrete prototype implementations, excluding its constructor
and six abstract declarations; the intake owner has17. These fixed definitions
must replace the old21-member driver row in the static-owner enumeration. They
are not per-instance fields or execution capacity. Current intake cells have
five fields plus their object; driver cells have four plus their object; the

Each live cell still needs its actual bound handlers, argument/reference cells,
